# Graph Report - BioDockify-Pharma-AI  (2026-07-19)

## Corpus Check
- 3603 files · ~6,010,820 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.

## Summary
- 40327 nodes · 75663 edges · 2915 communities (2318 shown, 597 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 91% EXTRACTED · 9% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 6953 edges (avg confidence: 0.58)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `6ac431f5`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
